连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

&lt;/project_context>
紧急：检查 autodl-5090 上 4 块 GPU 的状态，收集已完成实验的结果。

**步骤：**

1. 全局状态检查（ssh_read_only）：
   ```
   nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,memory.total,utilization.gpu --format=csv,noheader
   tmux ls
   ```

2. 检查 25% s7 eval（应该已完成，约 30 分钟前启动）：

   **cuda:2: v4-taxei-fixed-inject25-s7-eval-r2 (PID 210065)**
   - `ps -p 210065 -o pid= 2>/dev/null && echo RUNNING || echo DONE`
   - 如果 DONE：`cat /root/tax_error_inject_agent/eval_results/v4_taxei_fixed_inject25_s7_eval.json` 或 `tail -30 ~/runs/tax_error_inject_agent/v4-taxei-fixed-inject25-s7-eval-r2.log`
   - 提取 success_rate, successes, total, by_task_type

   **cuda:3: v4-random-fixed-inject25-s7-eval (PID 209729)**
   - `ps -p 209729 -o pid= 2>/dev/null && echo RUNNING || echo DONE`
   - 如果 DONE：`cat /root/tax_error_inject_agent/eval_results/v4_random_fixed_inject25_s7_eval.json` 或 `tail -30 ~/runs/tax_error_inject_agent/v4-random-fixed-inject25-s7-eval.log`
   - 提取 success_rate, successes, total, by_task_type

3. 检查 50% 训练进度：

   **cuda:0: v4-random-fixed-inject50-s1**
   - `tail -5 /tmp/v4-random-fixed-inject50-s1.log` 或搜索 tmux 日志找 step/loss
   - 报告当前 step/total_steps, loss, ETA

4. 对已完成的 eval，更新 registry：
   - 设 metrics（含 success_rate, successes, total, by_task_type）

5. 汇总报告格式：

   - cuda:1: [任务] [状态] [进度]

   - by_task_type: look_at_obj_in_light: 5/6 (0.833), pick_and_place_simple: 3/8 (0.375), pick_clean_then_place_in_recep: 5/11 (0.455), pick_cool_then_place_in_recep: 3/7 (0.429), pick_heat_then_place_in_recep: 6/8 (0.75), pick_two_obj_and_place: 5/7 (0.714)

2. **v4-random-fixed-inject25-s7-eval**: success_rate = 0.596 (28/47), slightly outperforming the first model. Breaking down by task type, it excels at pick_and_place_simple (0.625) and pick_clean_then_place_in_recep (0.545), though both models tie on pick_heat_then_place_in_recep at 0.75.

The training runs both hit issues — one ran out of GPU memory at step 5, and the other failed due to an invalid model repository ID and missing checkpoint directory. I need to update the registry with these eval results and mark the training failures, though I'm noticing the taxei eval saved its results under a different filename than expected, so I'll need to handle that mapping.

_source line 109 · 2026-05-15T23:55:20.184Z_

Cron 已不存在（可能已被之前的 session 清理）。全部 registry 更新完毕。

## GPU 状态汇总

| GPU | 任务 | 状态 | 结果 |
|-----|------|------|------|
| cuda:0 | v4-random-fixed-inject50-s1 (训练) | **FAILED** | OOM at step 5/1635 — `Tried to allocate 4.25 GiB`，batch_size=8 + max_length=4096 对 50% 数据集太大 |
| cuda:1 | v4-taxei-fixed-inject50-s1 (训练) | **FAILED** | ModelScope 报错 `Invalid repo_id: model`——swift sft 的 `--model Qwen3-8B` 缺少完整路径，应为 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen3-8B` |
| cuda:2 | v4-taxei-fixed-inject25-s7-eval-r2 | **DONE** | **success_rate = 57.4%** (27/47) |
| cuda:3 | v4-random-fixed-inject25-s7-eval | **DONE** | **success_rate = 59.6%** (28/47) |

### 25% s7 Eval 详细对比

| task_type | TaxEI (s7) | Random (s7) |
|-----------|-----------|-------------|
| look_at_obj_in_light | 5/6 (83.3%) | 4/6 (66.7%) |
| pick_and_place_simple | 3/8 (37.5%) | 5/8 (62.5%) |
| pick_clean_then_place | 5/11 (45.5%) | 6/11 (54.5%) |
| pick_cool_then_place | 3/7 (42.9%) | 3/7 (42.9%) |
| pick_heat_then_place | 6/8 (75.0%) | 6/8 (75.0%) |
| pick_two_obj_and_place | 5/7 (71.4%) | 4/7 (57.1%) |

### 50% 训练失败原因

- **cuda:0 (random)**: CUDA OOM — `per_device_train_batch_size=8` 配合 50% 数据集（4358条）溢出。需降到 batch_size=4 或加 gradient_checkpointing
- **cuda:1 (taxei)**: `--model Qwen3-8B` 被 ModelScope 解析为 repo_id 失败。命令中缺少完整模型路径 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen3-8B`

4 块 GPU 全部空闲，可重新调度 50% 训练。
